`optimize_with_beta(a, n, M, beta, max_iter, time_limit, eta0, step_scale)`:
- a normalized copy; f_fast = fast_score? We'll compute fval initial.
- eta = eta0. For it in range:
   if time exceeded break.
   fval, score, grad_z = compute...
   if not finite break.
   maxg = np.max(np.abs(grad_z)); if maxg < tiny: break (stabilized).
   scale = step_scale / maxg  # effective eta*grad_z in [-step_scale, step_scale].
   # This scale is eta. Because update factor exp(-eta grad_z) with eta=scale yields exponent ∈[-step_scale,step_scale].
   For bs in range(line_iters):
       logfac = -eta*grad_z
       logfac = np.clip(logfac, -50, 50)
       a_trial = a * np.exp(logfac)
       denom=a_trial.sum(); if not finite or denom<=0: eta*=0.5; continue
       a_trial = a_trial/denom
       # compute fval_trial maybe with same beta. Use function that only returns fval to save? We'll reuse compute.
       fval_trial,... = compute(...)
       if fval_trial < fval - 1e-12:
           accept; fval=fval_trial; score=score_trial? We can recompute score maybe included. Update a.
           # Increase eta slightly if line search succeeded first/second iteration: eta = min(max_eta, eta*1.2)
           break
       eta *= 0.5
   else: # no accept after max line and too small eta
       if eta < min_scale/maxg: break
- Return a,best_score_fast.

def multiplicative_update(a, g, scale):
    maxg = np.max(g)
    if maxg <= eps: return None, eta
    eta = scale / maxg
    for _ in range(8):
       logfac = -eta*g
       logfac = np.clip(logfac, -50, 50)
       a_new = a*np.exp(logfac)
       s=a_new.sum()
       if s>0 finite: a_new/=s
       f_new=fast_fval(...)
       if f_new < f_old*(1-1e-10): accept
       eta *= 0.5
    ...
```

def try_update(a,g,eta_scale,metric_func,beta? maybe):
    maxg=np.max(g)
    if maxg<=1e-30: return None,None
    eta = eta_scale / maxg
    for _ in range(8):
       af = a*np.exp(np.clip(-eta*g,-50,50))
       s=af.sum()
       if finite: af/=s
       metrics = metric(a_trial)
       if metrics better than old:
          return af, metrics
       eta*=0.5
    return None None
```

Line search metric functions:
```
def fast_score(a,n,M):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,M)[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    s=a.sum()
    return float(2*n*b.max()/(s*s))
```
Since a normalized, s=1. For fval logsumexp:
```
def fast_fval_log(a,n,M,beta):
   fa,b
   m=b.max()
   e=np.exp(beta*(b-m))
   z=e.sum()
   return (2*n/beta)*(np.log(z)+beta*m)
```
This fval approx score. But for line search, if beta small, fval difference might be tiny; use score.

`fast_fval_p(a,n,M,p)`:
```
fa,b; return float(np.mean(b**p))  # or sum
```
Mean avoids scale.

Gradient log:
```
def grad_log(a,n,M,beta):
   fa,b
   m=b.max(); e=np.exp(beta*(b-m)); s=e/e.sum()
   fa_s=np.fft.rfft(s,M)
   g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(fa_s*fa,M)[:n]
   g=np.maximum(g,0)
   return g, fval, score
```
Gradient p:
```
w=b**(p-1)
g=2.0*np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w,M)*fa,M)[:n]
```
For p=2, w=b; fval=mean(b^2). For p=4, w=b^3.

But derivative of mean(b^p) is p b^{p-1}; direction scale p irrelevant. Good.

Multiplicative update implementation details:
```
def do_update(a,g,eta):
    logfac = -eta*g
    # To center exponent to avoid overflow/renorm, subtract maybe median? Constant cancels; but if g has large mean, all decrease then normalize; same. We can subtract min? no effect. To reduce exponent range, we can scale eta by maxg already; logfac ∈[-eta*maxg,0] if g≥0. If maxg scaled to step_scale, minimum exponent -step_scale. Good. No need clip unless step_scale > 50.
    af = a*np.exp(logfac)
    s=af.sum()
    if not finite: af=np.nan
    else: af/=s
    return af
```
If we want allow increase some coordinates beyond 1, exponent 0 max, all coordinates only decrease then normalize; increase relative by factor 1/(1-Δ). The maximum relative increase for coordinate with g=0 compared to one with g=max is exp(eta*maxg)=e^{step_scale}. If step_scale 1, low-g can increase up to e=2.718 relative. Good.

def apply_mull(a,g,eta):

Since constant cancels, we can subtract g.min() to set max exponent 0. This keeps exp in [exp(-eta*(g-min)),1]. Good.
    gm = g.min()
    logfac = -eta*(g-gm)
    logfac = np.clip(logfac, -50, 0) # since g>=gm
    af = a*np.exp(logfac)
    s=af.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None
    af /= s
    return af
```

Now, local block perturb after gradient. Implement:
```
def block_search(a,best_score,n,M,deadline,rng):
    current=a.copy(); current_score=best_score
    local_best=current; local_score=best_score
    it=0
    while time < deadline:
        cand=current.copy()
        # Perturb 2-8 blocks
        nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
        for _ in range(nblocks):
             width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//2000), max(2,n//20)))
             start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
             stop=min(n,start+width)
             sigma = float(rng.choice([0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3]))
             cand[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        # Maybe small global ramp? no
        cand=np.clip(cand,0.0,None); s=cand.sum(); if s>0: cand/=s
        score=fast_score(cand,n,M)
        if score < local_score - 1e-12:
             local_score=score; current=cand; update global best callback?
        # else maybe keep current if annealing? no
        it+=1
        if it%500==0: print
    return current, local_score
```
This is simple hill climbing. It may get stuck; gradient polished. Could also accept slightly worse with restart? Not needed.

Smooth perturb for restart:
```
def smooth_perturb(a, rng, n, amp):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    off=np.zeros(n)
    for f in range(1,6):
       off += rng.normal(0,amp/(f**0.5))*np.sin(np.pi*f*t)
    # optional one tail ramp
    off += rng.normal(0,amp)*smoothstep((t-0.6)/0.4)
    cand=a*np.exp(clip(off,-2,2)); normalize
```
Use in main if stagnation.

`next_power_two(m)`:

def next_power_two(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

def fast_score(a,n,M):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, M)
    L=2*n-1
    if b.size < L: b=np.concatenate? M >=L.
    b = b[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    return float(2.0*n*b.max()/(s*s))
```

```
def metric(a,n,M,kind,param):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, M)[:2*n-1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    score = float(2.0*n*b.max()/(s*s))
    if kind == 'score':
        return score, score
    if kind == 'p':
        if param == 2:
            fval = float(np.mean(b*b))
        elif param == 4:
            bb = b*b
            fval = float(np.mean(bb*bb))
        else:
            fval = float(np.mean(np.power(b, param)))
        return score, fval
    # ls
    m = b.max()
    x = param * (b - m)
    # x <= 0 unless tiny
    x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    z = float(e.sum())
    if z <=0 or not finite:
       # fallback uniform over max
       fval = score
    else:
       fval = float((2.0*n/param)*(np.log(z) + param*m/(s*s)? wait if not normalized? For score scale, b scales. Our metric expects normalized? We normalize a, but if not, fval should include /s^2? `compute_grad`:
```
def compute_grad(a,n,M,kind,param):
    fa=b...
    if kind=='p':
       if param==2: w=b; fval=mean(b*b)
       elif param==4: w=b*b*b; fval=mean(w*b)? Actually b^4 mean = mean((b^2)^2). Could compute.
       else: w=np.power(b,param-1); fval=mean(np.multiply(w,b)) # b^p
       g=2.0*irfft(rfft(w,M)*fa,M)[:n]
    elif kind=='ls':
       m=b.max(); x=param*(b-m); x=np.minimum(x,0)
       e=np.exp(x); z=e.sum()
       if z>0: s=e/z; fval=(2*n/param)*(log(z)+param*m) else...
       g=2*irfft(rfft(s,M)*fa,M)[:n]
    g=np.maximum(g,0)
    return g,score,fval
```
For p=4, `w = b*b*b`, `fval=mean(w*b)`.
For p=2, `w=b`, fval=mean(b*b).
For p maybe 3? Not needed.

Phase code:
```
def run_gradient_phase(a,n,M,end_time,kind,param,step_scale,max_step,min_step,no_iter_limit):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    best_score = float(fast_score(a,n,M))
    best_a = a.copy()
    current = a
    current_score=best_score
    _, current_fval = metric(current,n,M,kind,param)
    step=step_scale
    stall=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
       g, cur_score, cur_fval = compute_grad(current,n,M,kind,param)
       if not np.isfinite(cur_score): break
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=cur_score; best_a=current.copy()
       maxg = np.max(g); ming = np.min(g)
       rangeg = maxg - ming
       if rangeg <= 1e-14:
           break
       eta = step / rangeg
       accepted=False
       for _ in range(7):
           a_new = apply_update(current,g,eta)
           if a_new is None:
               eta*=0.5; continue
           sc, fv = metric(a_new,n,M,kind,param)
           if finite:
               tol = max(1e-12, 1e-7*abs(old_fval?))
               if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                   accept
               elif fv < cur_fval - max(1e-12, 1e-6*abs(cur_fval)) and sc <= max(cur_score,best_score)*1.002:
                   accept
           eta*=0.5
           if eta < min_step / rangeg: break
       if accepted:
           current=a_new; current_score=sc; current_fval=fv; stall=0
           if current_score < best_score - 1e-12: best_score=current_score; best_a=current.copy()
           step = min(max_step, step*1.08)
       else:
           step = max(min_step, step*0.6)
           stall +=1
           if stall > 150: break
       it +=1
       if it % 200 == 0:
           print(...)
    return best_a,best_score
```
Need `old` variables: use `cur_score` and `cur_fval` from compute. In line search if accepted, `sc` defined. Use `accepted_new` etc.

Metric for kind param called many times. For kind='score', fval=score; acceptance fval same. But we use kind p/ls. Good.

Let's code `compute_b` carefully:
```
def convolution_state(a,n,M):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, M)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if b.size > L:
        b = b[:L]
    else:
        b = np.resize(b, L) # shouldn't
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return fa, b
```
`np.resize` may hide bug but okay. M >=L.

```
def smoothstep01(x):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x*x*(3.0-2.0*x)
```

`smooth_perturb`:
```
def smooth_perturb(a, rng, n, amp):
    t = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    off = np.zeros(n)
    for freq in range(1,6):
        off += rng.normal(0.0, amp/(freq+1.0))*np.cos(np.pi*freq*t)
    # add smooth ramp with random sign
    amp2 = amp*0.5
    ramp = smoothstep01((t-0.6)/0.4)
    off += amp2*ramp
    off = np.clip(off, -2.0, 2.0)
    c = a*np.exp(off)
    s = c.sum()
    if s>0 and finite: return c/s
    return a.copy()
```
This may change shape.

`add_candidate` no lambda:
```
def add_candidate(cands, score, a, maxlen=8):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    if len(cands) < maxlen:
        cands.append((float(score), np.array(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    else:
        worst_i=0; worst_score=-np.inf
        for i in range(len(cands)):
            if cands[i][0] > worst_score:
                worst_score=cands[i][0]; worst_i=i
        if score < worst_score:
            cands[worst_i] = (float(score), np.array(a, dtype=np.float64).copy())
```
But if score slightly better, copy. Good.

`sort_candidates` no lambda:
```
def sort_candidates(cands):
    for i in range(len(cands)-1):
       best=i
       for j in range(i+1,len(cands)):
          if cands[j][0] < cands[best][0]: best=j
       if best!=i: cands[i],cands[best]=cands[best],cands[i]
```

def metric(a,n,M,kind,param):
    fa,b = convolution_state(a,n,M)
    score = float(2.0 * n * b.max())  # assumes sum 1
    # optionally if sum not 1:
    s = a.sum()
    if s > 0: score = 2.0*n*b.max()/(s*s)
    if kind == 'score' or kind is None:
       return score, score
    if kind == 'p':
       if param == 2: w=b; fval=float(np.mean(b*b))
       elif param == 4: bb=b*b; w=bb*b; fval=float(np.mean(bb*bb))?
             if b*b is bb, b^4=bb*bb.
       else: w=np.power(b,param-1); fval=float(np.mean(w*b))
       return score,fval
    # ls
    bm=b.max()
    if s > 0: # normalize? use b/s^2 for fval
       bn = b/(s*s)
       bm=bn.max()
    else: bn=b
    x=param*(bn-bm)
    x=np.minimum(x,0.0)
    e=np.exp(x)
    z=float(e.sum())
    if z>0 and np.isfinite(z):
       fval=float((2.0*n/param)*(np.log(z)+param*bm))
    else:
       fval=score
    return score,fval
```

Let's write `run_gradient_phase` code carefully:
```
def run_gradient_phase(a, n, M, end_time, kind, param, initial_step, max_step, min_step, max_stall):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    best_score = float(fast_score(a,n,M))
    best_a = a.copy()
    current = a
    try:
       _, cur_fval = metric(current,n,M,kind,param)
    except Exception:
       return best_a, best_score
    cur_score = best_score
    step = initial_step
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        try:
            g, cur_score, cur_fval = compute_grad(current,n,M,kind,param)
        except Exception:
            break
        if not np.isfinite(cur_score): break
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score; best_a = current.copy()
        if g.size == 0: break
        maxg = float(np.max(g)); ming = float(np.min(g))
        rangeg = maxg - ming
        if not np.isfinite(rangeg) or rangeg <= 1e-14:
            break
        eta = step / rangeg
        accepted = False
        accepted_arr = None; accepted_score = cur_score; accepted_fval=cur_fval
        for _ in range(8):
            arr = apply_update(current, g, eta)
            if arr is None:
                eta *= 0.5; continue
            try:
                sc, fv = metric(arr,n,M,kind,param)
            except Exception:
                sc = np.inf; fv = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(sc) and np.isfinite(fv):
                score_ok = sc < cur_score - 1e-12
                f_tol = max(1e-11, 1e-6 * abs(cur_fval))
                f_ok = fv < cur_fval - f_tol
                cap = max(cur_score, best_score) * 1.002
                if score_ok or (f_ok and sc <= cap):
                    accepted=True; accepted_arr=arr; accepted_score=sc; accepted_fval=fv
                    break
            eta *= 0.5
            if eta < min_step / rangeg:
                break
        if accepted:
            current = accepted_arr
            cur_score = accepted_score
            cur_fval = accepted_fval
            stall = 0
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = cur_score; best_a = current.copy()
            step = min(max_step, step * 1.07)
        else:
            step = max(min_step, step * 0.6)
            stall += 1
            if stall > max_stall:
                break
        it += 1
        if it % 250 == 0:
            print("phase kind %s param %.4g score %.10f step %.4g" % (kind, param, best_score, step))
    if current_score? At loop end maybe current may be worse than best; return best.
    return best_a, best_score
```
Potential issue: We don't update `cur_score` after no accept, remains. Fine.
If after accepted, current_score improved, best update. If only fval accepted with score slightly worse but within cap, best not update. Next compute uses current.

For kind p, fval maybe tiny, f_tol 1e-11 might be larger than typical changes? old fval ~ mean b^2 ~ for n=8192, b~1e-5? Actually normalized b max 9e-5, mean ~6e-5, square mean ~ maybe 4e-9. f_tol 1e-11 (0.25%) okay. Changes may be 1e-11. Good.
For log fval ~1.5, f_tol 1e-6. Good.

`apply_update` uses eta; if eta very large? step max maybe 2.5, rangeg small can eta huge? If rangeg=1e-6, step/range=2.5e6; logfac clip -50, all exp(-50) maybe, but update extremely coarse. However if rangeg small, line search may fail; better before update, if rangeg < 1e-8 break? We set <=1e-14. If rangeg=1e-6, exponent range clipped at 50, not representing step. Could be okay? But if range small, improvements tiny; break if rangeg <1e-7 to save. Let's set `if rangeg <= 1e-8: break`. Since g values ~1, this means convergence. Use 1e-8.

In line search, if eta huge due range small, `apply_update` logfac = -eta*(g-min), for differences ~1e-6, product ~2.5 if eta=2.5/1e-6? Wait if range=1e-6, eta=2.5e6, exponent range=eta*range=2.5, okay because (g-min) max=range. Good. No issue.

In `apply_update`, using `np.min(g)` O(n). Already computed range. We can pass shift to avoid. Modify signature `apply_update(a,g,eta,shift=None)`; use `if shift is None: shift=np.min(g)`. In phase, `ming` known. Use `apply_update(current,g,eta,ming)`. Helper top-level.
```
def apply_update(a,g,eta,shift):
   logfac = -eta * (g - shift)
   logfac = np.clip(logfac, -50.0, 0.0)
   arr = a * np.exp(logfac)
   arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-300)
   s=arr.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return None
   return arr/s
```
Since g≥shift, logfac≤0. If all g equal, logfac=0, arr normalized same. Good.

Now, `metric` and `compute_grad` should ensure a finite. If not, return inf.

`convolution_state` if a contains nan? normalize prevents. But in compute_grad, before FFT check `if not np.all(np.isfinite(a))`? Too costly. We can assume.

Now, `block_search` code:
```
def block_search(a,best_score,n,M,end_time,rng):
    current = normalize_seq(a)
    cur_score = float(fast_score(current,n,M))
    it=0
    no=0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        cand = current.copy()
        # transfer or blocks
        if rng.random() < 0.45:
            # mass transfer from left to right
            i1 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n//2)))
            i2 = int(rng.integers(n//2, n))
            w1 = int(rng.integers(max(1, n//4000), max(2, n//100)))
            w2 = ...
            stop1=min(n,i1+w1); stop2=...
            delta=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.25))
            cand[i1:stop1] *= float(np.exp(-delta))
            # scale transfer to conserve roughly: delta2 = delta * (stop1-i1)/(stop2-i2)
            if stop2>i2:
                delta2 = delta * float(stop1-i1)/float(stop2-i2)
                cand[i2:stop2] *= float(np.exp(delta2))
        else:
            nb = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
            for _ in range(nb):
                w = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//3000), max(2,n//30)))
                st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-w+1)))
                sig=float(rng.choice((0.03,0.08,0.15,0.25)))
                cand[st:st+w] *= float(np.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0,sig))))
        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        s = cand.sum()
        if s <=0 or not finite: continue
        cand /= s
        sc = fast_score(cand,n,M)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
            current = cand; cur_score = sc; no=0
            if sc < best_score: update? Need pass global? We can return best. Also maybe print.
        else:
            no +=1
            if no > 2000: break
        it +=1
        if it % 1000 ==0: print
    return current, cur_score
```

def sort_candidates(cands):
    # in place
    L=len(cands)
    for i in range(L-1):
       best=i
       for j in range(i+1,L):
          if cands[j][0] < cands[best][0]: best=j
       if best != i:
          tmp=cands[i]; cands[i]=cands[best]; cands[best]=tmp
    return cands
```

Let's craft final answer.
</think>

```python
"""
Optimization strategy
---------------------
Minimize
    score(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
over non-negative sequences a of length n.

The construction is based on the important observation that the infinite
sequence C_i = binom(2i,i)/4^i satisfies

    sum_j C_i C_{j-i} = 1   for all j >= 0,

i.e. its infinite convolution is perfectly flat.  Its finite truncation
therefore has many leading convolution coefficients exactly equal, giving a
score close to pi/2.  The program starts from this one-sided central-binomial
profile and refines it by:

* fast FFT evaluations / gradients;
* multiplicative gradient steps for L2, L4, and log-sum-exp
  approximations of the maximum;
* random smooth restarts and block-mass-transfer local search;
* final validation with the权威 evaluator.

The profile is kept on a fixed length n=8192, which gives a good balance
between approximation quality and computing time.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def next_power_two(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_seq(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, arr.size)
    return arr / s


def smoothstep01(x):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x * x * (3.0 - 2.0 * x)


def make_central(n):
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i] = c[i - 1] * float(2 * i - 1) / float(2 * i)
    return c


def convolution_state(a, n, nfft):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfft)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if b.size < L:
        b = np.resize(b, L)
    else:
        b = b[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return fa, b


def fast_score(a, n, nfft):
    fa, b = convolution_state(a, n, nfft)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()) / (s * s))


def metric(a, n, nfft, kind, param):
    fa, b = convolution_state(a, n, nfft)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, np.inf
    diff = s * s
    bn = b / diff
    score = float(2.0 * n * float(bn.max()))

    if kind is None or kind == "score":
        return score, score

    if kind == "p":
        p = int(param)
        if p == 2:
            fval = float(np.mean(bn * bn))
        elif p == 4:
            bb = bn * bn
            fval = float(np.mean(bb * bb))
        else:
            fval = float(np.mean(np.power(bn, p)))
        return score, fval

    if kind == "ls":
        beta = float(param)
        bm = float(bn.max())
        x = beta * (bn - bm)
        x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(x)
        z = float(e.sum())
        if np.isfinite(z) and z > 0.0:
            fval = float((2.0 * n / beta) * (np.log(z) + beta * bm))
        else:
            fval = score
        return score, fval

    return score, score


def compute_grad(a, n, nfft, kind, param):
    fa, b = convolution_state(a, n, nfft)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf, np.inf
    diff = s * s
    bn = b / diff
    score = float(2.0 * n * float(bn.max()))

    if kind == "p":
        p = int(param)
        if p == 2:
            w = bn
            if p == 2:
                fval = float(np.mean(w * w))
        elif p == 4:
            w = bn * bn * bn
            bb = bn * bn
            fval = float(np.mean(bb * bb))
        else:
            w = np.power(bn, p - 1)
            fval = float(np.mean(np.power(bn, p)))
        g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w, nfft) * fa, nfft)[:n]
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        return g, score, fval

    if kind == "ls":
        beta = float(param)
        bm = float(bn.max())
        x = beta * (bn - bm)
        x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(x)
        z = float(e.sum())
        if np.isfinite(z) and z > 0.0:
            weights = e / z
            fval = float((2.0 * n / beta) * (np.log(z) + beta * bm))
        else:
            weights = np.ones_like(bn)
            if weights.size:
                weights /= float(weights.size)
            fval = score
        g = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(weights, nfft) * fa, nfft)[:n]
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        return g, score, fval

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return g, score, score


def apply_update(a, g, eta, shift):
    if not np.isfinite(eta) or eta <= 0.0:
        return None
    logfac = -eta * (g - shift)
    logfac = np.clip(logfac, -50.0, 0.0)
    arr = a * np.exp(logfac)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-300)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr / s


def run_gradient_phase(a, n, nfft, end_time, kind, param,
                       initial_step, max_step, min_step, max_stall):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    best_score = float(fast_score(a, n, nfft))
    best_a = a.copy()

    current = a
    try:
        cur_score, cur_fval = metric(current, n, nfft, kind, param)
    except Exception:
        return best_a, best_score

    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        cur_score = best_score
        cur_fval = best_score

    step = float(initial_step)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        try:
            g, cur_score, cur_fval = compute_grad(current, n, nfft, kind, param)
        except Exception:
            break

        if g is None or not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            break

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = current.copy()

        maxg = float(np.max(g))
        ming = float(np.min(g))
        rangeg = maxg - ming
        if not np.isfinite(rangeg) or rangeg <= 1e-8:
            break

        eta = step / rangeg
        accepted = False
        accepted_arr = None
        accepted_score = cur_score
        accepted_fval = cur_fval

        for _ in range(8):
            arr = apply_update(current, g, eta, ming)
            if arr is None:
                eta *= 0.5
                continue

            try:
                sc, fv = metric(arr, n, nfft, kind, param)
            except Exception:
                sc, fv = np.inf, np.inf

            if np.isfinite(sc) and np.isfinite(fv):
                score_ok = sc < cur_score - 1e-12
                f_tol = max(1e-11, 1e-6 * abs(cur_fval))
                f_ok = fv < cur_fval - f_tol
                cap = max(cur_score, best_score) * 1.0005
                if score_ok or (f_ok and sc <= cap):
                    accepted = True
                    accepted_arr = arr
                    accepted_score = sc
                    accepted_fval = fv
                    break

            eta *= 0.5
            if eta < max(min_step / max(rangeg, 1e-15), 1e-15):
                break

        if accepted:
            current = accepted_arr
            cur_score = accepted_score
            cur_fval = accepted_fval
            stall = 0
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_a = current.copy()
            step = min(float(max_step), step * 1.07)
        else:
            step = max(float(min_step), step * 0.6)
            stall += 1
            if stall > int(max_stall):
                break

        it += 1
        if it % 250 == 0:
            print("gradient phase kind=%s param=%.6g score=%.12f step=%.6g" %
                  (kind, param, best_score, step))

    return best_a, best_score


def smooth_perturb(a, rng, n, amp):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    off = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for freq in range(1, 6):
        off += float(rng.normal(0.0, amp / float(freq + 1))) * np.cos(np.pi * float(freq) * t)
    ramp = smoothstep01((t - 0.55) / 0.45)
    off += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * amp)) * ramp
    off = np.clip(off, -2.0, 2.0)
    c = a * np.exp(off)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        return c / s
    return a.copy()


def block_search(a, best_score, n, nfft, end_time, rng):
    current = normalize_seq(a)
    cur_score = float(fast_score(current, n, nfft))
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
        return current, best_score

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time:
        cand = current.copy()

        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            # Transfer mass from an earlier block to a later block.
            i1 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n // 2)))
            i2 = int(rng.integers(n // 2, n))
            w1 = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 4000), max(2, n // 120)))
            w2 = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 4000), max(2, n // 120)))
            s1 = i1
            t1 = min(n, i1 + w1)
            s2 = i2
            t2 = min(n, i2 + w2)

            if t1 > s1 and t2 > s2:
                delta = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.22))
                delta2 = delta * float(t1 - s1) / float(t2 - s2)
                cand[s1:t1] *= np.exp(-delta)
                cand[s2:t2] *= np.exp(delta2)
        else:
            nb = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
            for _ in range(nb):
                w = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 3000), max(2, n // 40)))
                st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
                sig = float(rng.choice((0.03, 0.08, 0.15, 0.25)))
                cand[st:st + w] *= np.exp(float(rng.normal(0.0, sig)))

        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand = cand / s

        sc = float(fast_score(cand, n, nfft))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
            current = cand
            cur_score = sc
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 3000:
                break

        it += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print("block search score=%.12f" % cur_score)

    return current, cur_score


def add_candidate(cands, score, a, maxlen=8):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if len(cands) < maxlen:
        cands.append((float(score), np.array(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    else:
        worst_i = 0
        worst_score = -np.inf
        for i in range(len(cands)):
            if cands[i][0] > worst_score:
                worst_score = cands[i][0]
                worst_i = i
        if score < worst_score:
            cands[worst_i] = (float(score), np.array(a, dtype=np.float64).copy())


def sort_candidates(cands):
    L = len(cands)
    for i in range(L - 1):
        best_i = i
        for j in range(i + 1, L):
            if cands[j][0] < cands[best_i][0]:
                best_i = j
        if best_i != i:
            tmp = cands[i]
            cands[i] = cands[best_i]
            cands[best_i] = tmp
    return cands


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1800.0

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, budget)
    search_deadline = start_time + total_budget - 40.0
    if search_deadline < start_time + 30.0:
        search_deadline = start_time + 30.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n = 8192
    nfft = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)

    # Basic construction: first n central-binomial coefficients.
    base = make_central(n)
    base = base / float(base.sum())

    cands = []
    add_candidate(cands, fast_score(base, n, nfft), base)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    # Choose a good one-dimensional tail-lifted initial point.
    initial_a = base.copy()
    initial_score = float(fast_score(initial_a, n, nfft))

    tail_specs = [
        (0.0, 0.0),
        (0.5, 0.5),
        (0.8, 0.7),
        (1.2, 0.8),
        (1.6, 0.85),
        (2.4, 0.92),
        (0.4, 0.35),
        (1.0, 0.55),
    ]
    for lam, st in tail_specs:
        if st < 1.0:
            u = smoothstep01((t - st) / max(1e-12, 1.0 - st))
        else:
            u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w = 1.0 + lambda_ * u if False else 1.0 + lam * u
        a = base * w
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        sc = float(fast_score(a, n, nfft))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < initial_score:
            initial_score = sc
            initial_a = a.copy()

    # A few combined early-reduction / tail-lift tries.
    combo_specs = [
        (0.05, 0.20, 0.50, 0.80),
        (0.08, 0.30, 0.70, 0.85),
        (0.04, 0.10, 0.30, 0.70),
        (0.10, 0.40, 1.00, 0.90),
    ]
    for red, red_st, lam, tail_st in combo_specs:
        if red > 0.0 and red_st > 0.0:
            u1 = smoothstep01(t / max(1e-12, red_st))
        else:
            u1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if tail_st < 1.0:
            u2 = smoothstep01((t - tail_st) / max(1e-12, 1.0 - tail_st))
        else:
            u2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w = (1.0 - red * u1) * (1.0 + lam * u2)
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-300)
        a = base * w
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        sc = float(fast_score(a, n, nfft))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < initial_score:
            initial_score = sc
            initial_a = a.copy()

    best_a = initial_a.copy()
    best_score = float(initial_score)
    add_candidate(cands, best_score, best_a)
    print("initial fast score=%.12f" % best_score)

    search_time = max(1.0, search_deadline - time.monotonic())
    fractions = [0.05, 0.04, 0.08, 0.09, 0.12, 0.18, 0.20, 0.14]

    phases = [
        ("p", 2, 0.30),
        ("p", 4, 0.30),
        ("ls", n / 5.0, 0.50),
        ("ls", n / 2.0, 0.50),
        ("ls", float(n), 0.60),
        ("ls", 5.0 * n, 0.75),
        ("ls", 20.0 * n, 0.95),
        ("ls", 50.0 * n, 1.20),
    ]

    for idx, (kind, param, step0) in enumerate(phases):
        if time.monotonic() >= search_deadline:
            break
        dur = fractions[idx] * search_time
        if dur < 5.0:
            dur = 5.0
        end = min(search_deadline, time.monotonic() + dur)
        if end - time.monotonic() < 1.0:
            continue

        a_out, sc_out = run_gradient_phase(
            best_a, n, nfft, end, kind, param,
            step0, 2.5, 1e-4, 150
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_a = a_out
            best_score = sc_out
            print("phase %s %.6g improved to %.12f" % (kind, param, best_score))
        add_candidate(cands, best_score, best_a)

    # A couple of smooth random restarts from the current best, if time allows.
    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 150.0:
        a2 = smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, n, 0.12)
        end2 = min(search_deadline, time.monotonic() + 100.0)
        a2, sc2 = run_gradient_phase(
            a2, n, nfft, end2, "ls", 20.0 * n,
            0.90, 2.5, 1e-4, 120
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_a = a2
            best_score = sc2
            print("restart 1 improved to %.12f" % best_score)
        add_candidate(cands, best_score, best_a)

    if time.monotonic() < search_deadline - 120.0:
        a3 = smooth_perturb(best_a, rng, n, 0.20)
        end3 = min(search_deadline, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
        a3, sc3 = run_gradient_phase(
            a3, n, nfft, end3, "ls", 5.0 * n,
            0.80, 2.5, 1e-4, 100
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_a = a3
            best_score = sc3
            print("restart 2 improved to %.12f" % best_score)
        add_candidate(cands, best_score, best_a)

    # Final local block/mass transfer search.
    if time.monotonic() + 5.0 < search_deadline:
        block_end = search_deadline - 2.0
        a_block, sc_block = block_search(
            best_a, best_score, n, nfft, block_end, rng
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_block) and sc_block < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_a = a_block
            best_score = sc_block
            print("block search improved to %.12f" % best_score)
        add_candidate(cands, best_score, best_a)

    # Validate a few candidates with the authoritative evaluator.
    sort_candidates(cands)
    best_seq = None
    best_exact = np.inf

    max_exact_evals = 5
    if len(cands) > max_exact_evals:
        eval_cands = cands[:max_exact_evals]
    else:
        eval_cands = cands

    for _, arr in eval_cands:
        if time.monotonic() > start_time + total_budget - 2.0:
            break
        seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)]
        try:
            es = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            es = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
            best_exact = es
            best_seq = seq

    # Also check the provided previous construction, if available.
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        if h is not None and time.monotonic() < start_time + total_budget - 1.0:
            seqh = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in h]
            try:
                esh = float(evaluate_sequence(seqh))
            except Exception:
                esh = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(esh) and esh < best_exact:
                best_exact = esh
                best_seq = seqh
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)]

    print("returned exact/fallback best_score=%.12f length=%d" %
          (best_exact if np.isfinite(best_exact) else best_score, len(best_seq)))

    return best_seq
```
